# Essay — Subject-Wide Practice Solutions

> Matching solutions for every question in the subject-wide workbook. These are repository-authored models, not official UPSC model answers.

## PART I — UPSC PYQ SOLUTIONS

### 2013-1 — Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji

**Verification:** V2 — carried-forward; re-check before verbatim use  
**Prompt type:** philosophical or quotation proposition

**Working thesis:** The prompt should be treated as a philosophical or quotation proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Complete argument map:**

1. At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct.
2. At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice.
3. At the institutional level, incentives and rules determine whether the insight becomes durable practice.
4. At the national level, law, policy and public capacity can enlarge benefits while controlling unequal risks.
5. At the global and intergenerational levels, the argument must account for interdependence, sustainability and responsibility.

**Model response:**

**Introduction.** “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji” presents a relationship that must be interpreted before it is illustrated. The essay should define its key terms, state a qualified position and keep every paragraph answerable to that position. The prompt should be treated as a philosophical or quotation proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Argument 1.** At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct. Applied to “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 2.** At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice. Applied to “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 3.** At the institutional level, incentives and rules determine whether the insight becomes durable practice. Applied to “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 4.** At the national level, law, policy and public capacity can enlarge benefits while controlling unequal risks. Applied to “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 5.** At the global and intergenerational levels, the argument must account for interdependence, sustainability and responsibility. Applied to “Be the change you want to see in others." — Gandhiji”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Counter-view and qualification.** The proposition should not be made universal merely because it is memorable. Its strongest exception may alter the scale, timing, institutional conditions or distribution of effects. A good counter-view therefore refines the thesis instead of replacing it with the opposite slogan.

**Conclusion.** The conclusion should return to the words of the prompt after the argument has deepened them. The final synthesis must join human agency with institutional responsibility and preserve both the insight and the limits established in the essay.

### 2013-4 — Science and Technology is the panacea for the growth and security of the nation.

**Verification:** V2 — carried-forward; re-check before verbatim use  
**Prompt type:** hybrid proposition

**Working thesis:** The prompt should be treated as a hybrid proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Complete argument map:**

1. Technology redistributes power, opportunity and risk; its effects depend on access, design, regulation and human agency.
2. Science advances through curiosity, doubt, evidence, reproducibility and institutions that permit correction.
3. Security is sustainable when prevention, capability, legitimacy, rights and public trust reinforce one another.
4. Growth becomes development when it creates productive work, public capability, resilience and fairly shared opportunity.
5. At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct.
6. At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice.

Applied to “Science and Technology is the panacea for the growth and security of the nation.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

Applied to “Science and Technology is the panacea for the growth and security of the nation.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 5.** At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct. **Prompt type:** hybrid proposition

**Working thesis:** The prompt should be treated as a hybrid proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Complete argument map:**

1. Gender norms distribute freedom, care, labour, safety and authority unequally across households, institutions and public life.
2. At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct.
3. At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice.
4. At the institutional level, incentives and rules determine whether the insight becomes durable practice.
5. At the national level, law, policy and public capacity can enlarge benefits while controlling unequal risks.
6. At the global and intergenerational levels, the argument must account for interdependence, sustainability and responsibility.

**Model response:**

**Introduction.** “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.” presents a relationship that must be interpreted before it is illustrated. The essay should define its key terms, state a qualified position and keep every paragraph answerable to that position. The prompt should be treated as a hybrid proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Argument 1.** Gender norms distribute freedom, care, labour, safety and authority unequally across households, institutions and public life. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 2.** At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 3.** At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 4.** At the institutional level, incentives and rules determine whether the insight becomes durable practice. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 5.** At the national level, law, policy and public capacity can enlarge benefits while controlling unequal risks. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 6.** At the global and intergenerational levels, the argument must account for interdependence, sustainability and responsibility. Applied to “If development is not engendered, it is endangered.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Counter-view and qualification.** The proposition should not be made universal merely because it is memorable. Its strongest exception may alter the scale, timing, institutional conditions or distribution of effects. A good counter-view therefore refines the thesis instead of replacing it with the opposite slogan.

**Conclusion.** The conclusion should return to the words of the prompt after the argument has deepened them. The final synthesis must join human agency with institutional responsibility and preserve both the insight and the limits established in the essay.

The essay should define its key terms, state a qualified position and keep every paragraph answerable to that position. The prompt should be treated as a hybrid proposition. A defensible answer should accept its central insight conditionally, explain the mechanism through which it operates, test it across scales, confront the strongest counter-case and end with an earned synthesis rather than an absolute claim.

**Argument 1.** At the individual level, the proposition affects agency, character, capability and everyday conduct. Applied to “The measure of inclusion is participation, not presence.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 2.** At the social level, it shapes relationships, norms, inclusion and the distribution of voice. Applied to “The measure of inclusion is participation, not presence.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 3.** At the institutional level, incentives and rules determine whether the insight becomes durable practice. Applied to “The measure of inclusion is participation, not presence.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 4.** At the national level, law, policy and public capacity can enlarge benefits while controlling unequal risks. Applied to “The measure of inclusion is participation, not presence.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Argument 5.** At the global and intergenerational levels, the argument must account for interdependence, sustainability and responsibility. Applied to “The measure of inclusion is participation, not presence.”, this dimension must be connected to a mechanism and a safe illustration rather than left as a decorative heading.

**Counter-view and qualification.** The proposition should not be made universal merely because it is memorable. Its strongest exception may alter the scale, timing, institutional conditions or distribution of effects. A good counter-view therefore refines the thesis instead of replacing it with the opposite slogan.

**Conclusion.** The conclusion should return to the words of the prompt after the argument has deepened them. The final synthesis must join human agency with institutional responsibility and preserve both the insight and the limits established in the essay.
